   "source": [
    "create a functions once and use it for grouping by and show some charts so i don't repeat codes many times"
   ]
  },
  {
   "cell_type": "code",
   "execution_count": 7,
   "id": "b18ea9ce",
   "metadata": {},
   "outputs": [],
   "source": [
    "def plotscount(columnsname,top10 = False):\n",
    "    prop = df.groupby(columnsname, as_index= False)['invoice'].count()\n",
    "    prop['proportion'] = (prop.invoice / df.total_sold_sheets.count())*100\n",
    "    prop = prop.sort_values('invoice', ascending = False)\n",
    "    \n",
    "    print('Describing The Total Amount')\n",
    "    \n",
    "    if top10 == True:\n",
    "        prop = prop.head(10)\n",
    "        \n",
    "    fig1 = px.bar(data_frame= prop, x = columnsname, y = 'invoice')\n",
    "    fig2 = px.pie(data_frame= prop, names= columnsname, values= 'invoice')\n",
    "    \n",
    "    \n",
    "    fig1.show()\n",
    "    fig2.show()"
   ]
  },

  {
   "cell_type": "code",
   "execution_count": 11,
   "id": "ed8b6072",
   "metadata": {},
   "outputs": [],
   "source": [
    "def plots(columnname,top10 = False):\n",
    "    prop = df.groupby(columnname, as_index= False)['total_sold_sheets'].sum()\n",
    "    prop['proportion'] = (prop.total_sold_sheets / df.total_sold_sheets.sum())*100\n",
    "    prop = prop.sort_values('total_sold_sheets', ascending = False)\n",
    "    \n",
    "    print('Describing The Total Amount')\n",
    "    \n",
    "    if top10 == True:\n",
    "        prop = prop.head(10)\n",
    "        \n",
    "    fig1 = px.bar(data_frame= prop, x = columnname, y = 'total_sold_sheets')\n",
    "    fig2 = px.pie(data_frame= prop, names = columnname, values= 'total_sold_sheets')\n",
    "    \n",
    "    fig1.show()\n",
    "    fig2.show()\n",
    "    "
   ]
  },
